### 3.9.5 MoE 娣卞害瑙ｆ瀽

#### MoE鐨勮礋杞藉潎琛￠棶棰?
```python
"""
MoE璐熻浇鍧囪　闂璇﹁В

闂鎻忚堪锛?濡傛灉闂ㄦ帶缃戠粶鎬绘槸閫夋嫨灏戞暟鍑犱釜涓撳锛屼細瀵艰嚧锛?1. 琚€変腑鐨勪笓瀹惰繃杞斤紝璁粌涓嶅厖鍒?2. 鏈閫変腑鐨勪笓瀹堕棽缃紝娴垂鍙傛暟
3. 妯″瀷鎬ц兘涓嬮檷

瑙ｅ喅鏂规锛氳緟鍔╂崯澶憋紙Auxiliary Loss锛?
璐熻浇鍧囪　鎹熷け鍏紡锛?    L_aux = 伪 * n * 危岬?f岬?* P岬?

鍏朵腑锛?- f岬?= 涓撳i琚€変腑鐨勯鐜?- P岬?= 涓撳i鐨勫钩鍧囪矾鐢卞垎鏁?- n = 涓撳鎬绘暟
- 伪 = 鎹熷け鏉冮噸

杩欎釜鎹熷け榧撳姳锛?1. 鎵€鏈変笓瀹惰鍧囧寑閫変腑锛坒岬㈡帴杩?/n锛?2. 鎵€鏈変笓瀹剁殑璺敱鍒嗘暟鐩歌繎锛圥岬㈡帴杩戝潎鍊硷級
"""

class LoadBalancingLoss(nn.Module):
    """
    璐熻浇鍧囪　鎹熷け
    """
    def __init__(self, n_experts, alpha=0.1):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.alpha = alpha
    
    def forward(self, router_probs, expert_indices):
        """
        璁＄畻璐熻浇鍧囪　鎹熷け
        
        鍙傛暟:
            router_probs: 璺敱姒傜巼 (batch * seq, n_experts)
            expert_indices: 閫変腑鐨勪笓瀹剁储寮?(batch * seq, top_k)
        
        杩斿洖:
            璐熻浇鍧囪　鎹熷け
        """
        # 璁＄畻姣忎釜涓撳琚€変腑鐨勯鐜?        expert_mask = F.one_hot(expert_indices, self.n_experts).float()
        expert_freq = expert_mask.sum(dim=(0, 1)) / expert_mask.numel() * self.n_experts
        
        # 璁＄畻姣忎釜涓撳鐨勫钩鍧囪矾鐢卞垎鏁?        expert_prob = router_probs.mean(dim=0)
        
        # 璐熻浇鍧囪　鎹熷け
        loss = self.alpha * self.n_experts * (expert_freq * expert_prob).sum()
        
        return loss
```

#### MoE鐨勯珮绾ц矾鐢辩瓥鐣?
```python
"""
MoE楂樼骇璺敱绛栫暐

1. Top-K璺敱锛堝熀纭€锛?   閫夋嫨鍒嗘暟鏈€楂樼殑K涓笓瀹?
2. 涓撳閫夋嫨璺敱锛圗xpert Choice锛?   璁╀笓瀹堕€夋嫨token锛岃€屼笉鏄痶oken閫夋嫨涓撳
   鑷姩瀹炵幇璐熻浇鍧囪　

3. Soft璺敱
   浣跨敤鎵€鏈変笓瀹剁殑鍔犳潈骞冲潎
   璁＄畻閲忓ぇ浣嗗钩婊?
4. 娣峰悎璺敱
   缁撳悎Top-K鍜孲oft璺敱鐨勪紭鐐?
5. 瀹归噺鍥犲瓙
   闄愬埗姣忎釜涓撳澶勭悊鐨則oken鏁伴噺
   瓒呭嚭瀹归噺鐨則oken璺宠繃鎴栭噸鏂拌矾鐢?"""

class ExpertChoiceRouter(nn.Module):
    """
    涓撳閫夋嫨璺敱
    璁╀笓瀹朵富鍔ㄩ€夋嫨瑕佸鐞嗙殑token
    """
    def __init__(self, dim, n_experts, top_k, capacity_factor=1.25):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.capacity_factor = capacity_factor
        
        self.gate = nn.Linear(dim, n_experts, bias=False)
    
    def forward(self, x):
        """
        涓撳閫夋嫨璺敱
        
        鍙傛暟:
            x: 杈撳叆寮犻噺 (batch, seq, dim)
        
        杩斿洖:
            璺敱鍚庣殑杈撳嚭
        """
        batch, seq, dim = x.shape
        
        # 璁＄畻璺敱鍒嗘暟
        router_logits = self.gate(x)  # (batch, seq, n_experts)
        
        # 杞疆锛氳涓撳缁村害鍦ㄥ墠
        router_logits = router_logits.transpose(0, 2)  # (n_experts, seq, batch)
        router_logits = router_logits.reshape(self.n_experts, -1)  # (n_experts, batch * seq)
        
        # 璁＄畻姣忎釜涓撳鐨勫閲?        capacity = int(self.capacity_factor * seq * batch / self.n_experts)
        
        # 姣忎釜涓撳閫夋嫨top-capacity涓猼oken
        top_k_scores, top_k_indices = torch.topk(
            router_logits, capacity, dim=-1
        )
        
        # 搴旂敤softmax
        top_k_scores = F.softmax(top_k_scores, dim=-1)
        
        return top_k_scores, top_k_indices


class SoftRouter(nn.Module):
    """
    Soft璺敱锛氫娇鐢ㄦ墍鏈変笓瀹剁殑鍔犳潈骞冲潎
    """
    def __init__(self, dim, n_experts):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.gate = nn.Linear(dim, n_experts, bias=False)
    
    def forward(self, x, experts):
        """
        Soft璺敱
        
        鍙傛暟:
            x: 杈撳叆寮犻噺 (batch, seq, dim)
            experts: 涓撳缃戠粶鍒楄〃
        
        杩斿洖:
            鍔犳潈骞冲潎杈撳嚭
        """
        # 璁＄畻璺敱鏉冮噸
        router_weights = F.softmax(self.gate(x), dim=-1)  # (batch, seq, n_experts)
        
        # 璁＄畻鎵€鏈変笓瀹剁殑杈撳嚭
        expert_outputs = []
        for expert in experts:
            expert_outputs.append(expert(x))
        
        # 鍫嗗彔涓撳杈撳嚭
        expert_outputs = torch.stack(expert_outputs, dim=-1)  # (batch, seq, dim, n_experts)
        
        # 鍔犳潈骞冲潎
        output = (expert_outputs * router_weights.unsqueeze(-2)).sum(dim=-1)
        
        return output
```

#### 鍏变韩涓撳鐨勮璁″師鐞?
```python
"""
鍏变韩涓撳锛圫hared Experts锛夌殑璁捐鍘熺悊

鍔ㄦ満锛?1. 鏌愪簺鐭ヨ瘑鏄€氱敤鐨勶紝鎵€鏈塼oken閮介渶瑕?2. 璺敱涓撳涓撴敞浜庣壒瀹氶鍩熺殑鐭ヨ瘑
3. 鍏变韩涓撳澶勭悊閫氱敤鐭ヨ瘑

瀹炵幇锛?- 鍏变韩涓撳瀵规墍鏈塼oken閮芥縺娲?- 杈撳嚭涓庤矾鐢变笓瀹惰緭鍑虹浉鍔?- 涓嶉渶瑕佽矾鐢卞喅绛?
浼樺娍锛?1. 鍑忓皯璺敱涓撳鐨勮礋鎷?2. 淇濈暀閫氱敤鐭ヨ瘑
3. 鎻愰珮妯″瀷绋冲畾鎬?
MiniMind鐨勯厤缃細
- 4涓矾鐢变笓瀹讹紙姣忎釜token婵€娲?涓級
- 1涓叡浜笓瀹讹紙鎵€鏈塼oken閮芥縺娲伙級
"""

class SharedExpertMoE(nn.Module):
    """
    甯﹀叡浜笓瀹剁殑MoE瀹炵幇
    """
    def __init__(self, dim, hidden_dim, n_routed_experts, n_shared_experts, top_k):
        super().__init__()
        
        self.n_routed_experts = n_routed_experts
        self.n_shared_experts = n_shared_experts
        self.top_k = top_k
        
        # 璺敱涓撳
        self.routed_experts = nn.ModuleList([
            FeedForward(dim, hidden_dim, multiple_of=64)
            for _ in range(n_routed_experts)
        ])
        
        # 鍏变韩涓撳
        self.shared_experts = nn.ModuleList([
            FeedForward(dim, hidden_dim, multiple_of=64)
            for _ in range(n_shared_experts)
        ])
        
        # 璺敱闂ㄦ帶
        self.gate = nn.Linear(dim, n_routed_experts, bias=False)
    
    def forward(self, x):
        batch, seq, dim = x.shape
        x_flat = x.view(-1, dim)
        
        # 璁＄畻璺敱鏉冮噸
        router_logits = self.gate(x_flat)  # (batch * seq, n_routed_experts)
        router_weights = F.softmax(router_logits, dim=-1)
        
        # 閫夋嫨top-k璺敱涓撳
        top_k_weights, top_k_indices = torch.topk(router_weights, self.top_k, dim=-1)
        top_k_weights = top_k_weights / top_k_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        
        # 鍒濆鍖栬緭鍑?        output = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
        
        # 璁＄畻璺敱涓撳杈撳嚭
        for i in range(self.top_k):
            expert_idx = top_k_indices[:, i]  # (batch * seq,)
            weight = top_k_weights[:, i:i+1]  # (batch * seq, 1)
            
            for e in range(self.n_routed_experts):
                mask = (expert_idx == e)
                if mask.any():
                    expert_input = x_flat[mask]
                    expert_output = self.routed_experts[e](expert_input)
                    output[mask] += weight[mask] * expert_output
        
        # 璁＄畻鍏变韩涓撳杈撳嚭
        shared_output = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
        for shared_expert in self.shared_experts:
            shared_output += shared_expert(x_flat)
        shared_output = shared_output / self.n_shared_experts
        
        # 鍚堝苟杈撳嚭
        output = output + shared_output
        
        return output.view(batch, seq, dim)
```

### 3.9.6 妯″瀷鍒濆鍖栦笌鏉冮噸鍏变韩

#### 鏉冮噸鍒濆鍖栫瓥鐣?
```python
"""
妯″瀷鏉冮噸鍒濆鍖栫殑閲嶈鎬?
濂界殑鍒濆鍖栧彲浠ワ細
1. 鍔犻€熸敹鏁?2. 閬垮厤姊害娑堝け/鐖嗙偢
3. 鎻愰珮鏈€缁堟€ц兘

甯哥敤鍒濆鍖栨柟娉曪細

1. Xavier/Glorot鍒濆鍖?   閫傜敤浜巗igmoid/tanh婵€娲?   std = sqrt(2 / (fan_in + fan_out))

2. Kaiming/He鍒濆鍖?   閫傜敤浜嶳eLU鍙婂叾鍙樹綋
   std = sqrt(2 / fan_in)

3. 姝ｆ€佸垎甯冨垵濮嬪寲
   Transformer甯哥敤
   std = 0.02 鎴?1/sqrt(dim)

4. 鎴柇姝ｆ€佸垎甯?   閬垮厤鏋佺鍊?   鍦╗-2*std, 2*std]鑼冨洿鍐呮埅鏂?"""

def init_weights(module, method='normal', std=0.02):
    """
    鏉冮噸鍒濆鍖栧嚱鏁?    
    鍙傛暟:
        module: 瑕佸垵濮嬪寲鐨勬ā鍧?        method: 鍒濆鍖栨柟娉?        std: 鏍囧噯宸?    """
    if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
        if method == 'normal':
            nn.init.normal_(module.weight, mean=0.0, std=std)
        elif method == 'xavier':
            nn.init.xavier_normal_(module.weight)
        elif method == 'kaiming':
            nn.init.kaiming_normal_(module.weight, mode='fan_in', nonlinearity='relu')
        
        if module.bias is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
    
    elif isinstance(module, nn.Embedding):
        nn.init.normal_(module.weight, mean=0.0, std=std)
    
    elif isinstance(module, nn.LayerNorm):
        nn.init.ones_(module.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
    
    elif isinstance(module, RMSNorm):
        nn.init.ones_(module.weight)


def scaled_init(module, dim, n_layers):
    """
    缂╂斁鍒濆鍖?    
    瀵逛簬娣卞眰缃戠粶锛屼娇鐢ㄨ緝灏忕殑鍒濆鍖栨柟宸?    std = 0.02 / sqrt(2 * n_layers)
    
    杩欐湁鍔╀簬绋冲畾娣卞眰缃戠粶鐨勮缁?    """
    std = 0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * n_layers)
    init_weights(module, std=std)
```

#### 鏉冮噸鍏变韩鐨勫師鐞?
```python
"""
鏉冮噸鍏变韩锛圵eight Tying锛夌殑鍘熺悊

鍦ㄨ瑷€妯″瀷涓紝杈撳叆宓屽叆灞傚拰杈撳嚭灞傚叡浜潈閲嶏細

杈撳叆宓屽叆灞傦細
    E: vocab_size 脳 dim
    灏唗oken ID鏄犲皠涓哄悜閲?
杈撳嚭灞傦細
    W: dim 脳 vocab_size
    灏嗛殣钘忕姸鎬佹槧灏勪负logits

鍏变韩鏉冮噸锛?    W = E^T
    鍗?output = hidden @ E^T

涓轰粈涔堝叡浜潈閲嶏紵

1. 鍙傛暟鏁堢巼
   鍑忓皯鍙傛暟閲忥細vocab_size 脳 dim
   瀵逛簬vocab_size=6400, dim=512锛氬噺灏戠害3.3M鍙傛暟

2. 姝ｅ垯鍖栨晥鏋?   鍏变韩鏉冮噸鐩稿綋浜庢坊鍔犱簡绾︽潫
   鏈夊姪浜庨槻姝㈣繃鎷熷悎

3. 璇箟涓€鑷存€?   杈撳叆鍜岃緭鍑洪兘琛ㄧずtoken
   搴旇鍦ㄥ悓涓€璇箟绌洪棿涓?
4. 瀹為獙楠岃瘉
   鍦ㄥ涓换鍔′笂锛屽叡浜潈閲嶆晥鏋滄洿濂?
娉ㄦ剰浜嬮」锛?- 鍏变韩鏉冮噸鍙兘涓嶉€傜敤浜庢墍鏈変换鍔?- 瀵逛簬鏌愪簺鐗瑰畾浠诲姟锛岀嫭绔嬫潈閲嶅彲鑳芥洿濂?"""

class WeightTiedLM(nn.Module):
    """
    甯︽潈閲嶅叡浜殑璇█妯″瀷
    """
    def __init__(self, vocab_size, dim):
        super().__init__()
        
        # 宓屽叆灞?        self.embedding = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, dim)
        
        # 鍏朵粬灞?..
        # ...
        
        # 杈撳嚭灞傦紙涓嶅垱寤烘柊鐨勬潈閲嶏級
        # 浣跨敤embedding鐨勬潈閲嶄綔涓鸿緭鍑烘潈閲?    
    def forward(self, input_ids):
        # 宓屽叆
        x = self.embedding(input_ids)
        
        # 閫氳繃鍏朵粬灞?..
        # x = self.layers(x)
        
        # 杈撳嚭logits
        # 浣跨敤embedding鏉冮噸杩涜璁＄畻
        logits = F.linear(x, self.embedding.weight)
        
        return logits
```

### 3.9.7 姊害娴佷笌鍙嶅悜浼犳挱

```python
"""
Transformer涓殑姊害娴佸垎鏋?
鐞嗚В姊害濡備綍鍦═ransformer涓祦鍔ㄥ浜庤皟璇曞拰浼樺寲鑷冲叧閲嶈

1. 娈嬪樊杩炴帴鐨勬搴︽祦
   y = x + f(x)
   鈭侺/鈭倄 = 鈭侺/鈭倅 + 鈭侺/鈭倅 * 鈭俧/鈭倄
   
   姊害鍙互鐩存帴閫氳繃娈嬪樊杩炴帴娴佸姩锛屼笉缁忚繃f
   杩欐湁鍔╀簬缂撹В姊害娑堝け

2. LayerNorm/RMSNorm鐨勬搴︽祦
   y = x / ||x|| * 纬
   
   姊害浼氶噸鏂板垎閰嶅埌鎵€鏈夌淮搴?   鏈夊姪浜庣ǔ瀹氳缁?
3. 娉ㄦ剰鍔涚殑姊害娴?   Attention(Q, K, V) = softmax(QK^T) * V
   
   姊害浠嶸鍙嶅悜浼犳挱鍒癚銆並
   Softmax鐨勬搴﹀彲鑳藉鑷存搴︽秷澶?
4. FFN鐨勬搴︽祦
   y = W2 * activation(W1 * x)
   
   婵€娲诲嚱鏁扮殑姊害褰卞搷姊害娴佸姩
   SwiGLU鐨勬搴︽瘮ReLU鏇村钩婊?"""

def analyze_gradient_flow(model, input_ids):
    """
    鍒嗘瀽妯″瀷鐨勬搴︽祦
    
    鍙傛暟:
        model: 璇█妯″瀷
        input_ids: 杈撳叆token ID
    
    杩斿洖:
        鍚勫眰鐨勬搴︾粺璁′俊鎭?    """
    # 鍓嶅悜浼犳挱
    outputs = model(input_ids)
    loss = outputs.logits.sum()
    
    # 鍙嶅悜浼犳挱
    loss.backward()
    
    # 鏀堕泦姊害缁熻淇℃伅
    gradient_stats = {}
    
    for name, param in model.named_parameters():
        if param.grad is not None:
            grad = param.grad
            stats = {
                'mean': grad.mean().item(),
                'std': grad.std().item(),
                'min': grad.min().item(),
                'max': grad.max().item(),
                'norm': grad.norm().item(),
            }
            gradient_stats[name] = stats
    
    return gradient_stats


def gradient_clipping_analysis(model, max_norm=1.0):
    """
    姊害瑁佸壀鍒嗘瀽
    
    鍙傛暟:
        model: 璇█妯″瀷
        max_norm: 鏈€澶ф搴﹁寖鏁?    
    杩斿洖:
        瑁佸壀鍓嶅悗鐨勬搴﹁寖鏁?    """
    # 璁＄畻鎬绘搴﹁寖鏁?    total_norm = 0.0
    for param in model.parameters():
        if param.grad is not None:
            param_norm = param.grad.data.norm(2)
            total_norm += param_norm.item() ** 2
    total_norm = total_norm ** 0.5
    
    # 璁＄畻瑁佸壀绯绘暟
    clip_coef = max_norm / (total_norm + 1e-6)
    
    if clip_coef < 1:
        # 闇€瑕佽鍓?        for param in model.parameters():
            if param.grad is not None:
                param.grad.data.mul_(clip_coef)
    
    return {
        'original_norm': total_norm,
        'clipped': clip_coef < 1,
        'clip_coef': clip_coef,
        'final_norm': min(total_norm, max_norm)
    }
```

---

### 3.9.8 妯″瀷鎬ц兘浼樺寲鎶€宸?
```python
"""
妯″瀷鎬ц兘浼樺寲鎶€宸?
1. 绠楀瓙铻嶅悎
   灏嗗涓搷浣滃悎骞朵负涓€涓紝鍑忓皯鍐呭瓨璁块棶

2. 姊害妫€鏌ョ偣
   浠ヨ绠楁崲鍐呭瓨锛屽噺灏戞樉瀛樺崰鐢?
3. 娣峰悎绮惧害璁粌
   浣跨敤float16/bfloat16鍔犻€熻绠?
4. 缂栬瘧浼樺寲
   浣跨敤torch.compile杩涜鍥句紭鍖?
5. 鍐呭瓨浼樺寲
   浣跨敤inplace鎿嶄綔锛屽噺灏戝唴瀛樺垎閰?"""

# 1. 绠楀瓙铻嶅悎绀轰緥
class FusedQKVProjection(nn.Module):
    """
    铻嶅悎鐨凲KV鎶曞奖
    灏嗕笁涓姇褰卞悎骞朵负涓€涓?    """
    def __init__(self, dim, n_heads, n_kv_heads):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_heads = n_heads
        self.n_kv_heads = n_kv_heads
        self.head_dim = dim // n_heads
        
        # 铻嶅悎鐨凲KV鎶曞奖
        total_dim = n_heads * self.head_dim + 2 * n_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.qkv_proj = nn.Linear(dim, total_dim, bias=False)
    
    def forward(self, x):
        batch, seq, _ = x.shape
        
        # 涓€娆℃€ц绠桻銆並銆乂
        qkv = self.qkv_proj(x)
        
        # 鍒嗙Q銆並銆乂
        q_dim = self.n_heads * self.head_dim
        kv_dim = self.n_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        
        q = qkv[:, :, :q_dim]
        k = qkv[:, :, q_dim:q_dim + kv_dim]
        v = qkv[:, :, q_dim + kv_dim:]
        
        return q, k, v


# 2. 姊害妫€鏌ョ偣绀轰緥
from torch.utils.checkpoint import checkpoint

class CheckpointedTransformerBlock(nn.Module):
    """
    甯︽搴︽鏌ョ偣鐨凾ransformer鍧?    浠ヨ绠楁崲鍐呭瓨
    """
    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__()
        self.attention = Attention(config)
        self.feed_forward = FeedForward(config.dim, config.hidden_dim, config.multiple_of)
        self.attention_norm = RMSNorm(config.dim)
        self.ffn_norm = RMSNorm(config.dim)
    
    def _forward(self, x, pos_cis):
        # 娉ㄦ剰鍔涘瓙灞?        h = x + self.attention(self.attention_norm(x), pos_cis)[0]
        # FFN瀛愬眰
        out = h + self.feed_forward(self.ffn_norm(h))
        return out
    
    def forward(self, x, pos_cis):
        # 浣跨敤姊害妫€鏌ョ偣
        return checkpoint(self._forward, x, pos_cis, use_reentrant=False)


# 3. 缂栬瘧浼樺寲绀轰緥
def compile_model(model):
    """
    浣跨敤torch.compile浼樺寲妯″瀷
    
    缂栬瘧浼樺寲鍖呮嫭锛?    - 绠楀瓙铻嶅悎
    - 姝讳唬鐮佹秷闄?    - 甯搁噺鎶樺彔
    - 鍐呭瓨甯冨眬浼樺寲
    """
    # 浣跨敤榛樿妯″紡
    compiled_model = torch.compile(model)
    
    # 鎴栬€呬娇鐢ㄦ洿婵€杩涚殑浼樺寲
    # compiled_model = torch.compile(model, mode='max-autotune')
    
    return compiled_model


# 4. 鍐呭瓨浼樺寲绀轰緥
class MemoryEfficientAttention(nn.Module):
    """
    鍐呭瓨楂樻晥鐨勬敞鎰忓姏瀹炵幇
    浣跨敤inplace鎿嶄綔鍜屽唴瀛樺鐢?    """
    def __init__(self, dim, n_heads):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_heads = n_heads
        self.head_dim = dim // n_heads
        self.scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        
        self.qkv_proj = nn.Linear(dim, 3 * dim, bias=False)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim, bias=False)
    
    def forward(self, x):
        batch, seq, dim = x.shape
        
        # 璁＄畻QKV
        qkv = self.qkv_proj(x)
        qkv = qkv.view(batch, seq, 3, self.n_heads, self.head_dim)
        qkv = qkv.permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
        q, k, v = qkv[0], qkv[1], qkv[2]
        
        # 浣跨敤Flash Attention锛堝鏋滃彲鐢級
        if hasattr(F, 'scaled_dot_product_attention'):
            out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
        else:
            # 鎵嬪姩瀹炵幇锛屼娇鐢ㄥ垎鍧楄绠楀噺灏戝唴瀛?            out = self._blockwise_attention(q, k, v)
        
        # 鍚堝苟杈撳嚭
        out = out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        out = out.view(batch, seq, dim)
        
        return self.out_proj(out)
    
    def _blockwise_attention(self, q, k, v, block_size=64):
        """鍒嗗潡娉ㄦ剰鍔涜绠?""
        batch, n_heads, seq, head_dim = q.shape
        
        output = torch.zeros_like(q)
        
        for i in range(0, seq, block_size):
            i_end = min(i + block_size, seq)
            q_block = q[:, :, i:i_end, :]
            
            # 璁＄畻褰撳墠鍧楃殑娉ㄦ剰鍔?            scores = torch.matmul(q_block, k.transpose(-2, -1)) * self.scale
            
            # 搴旂敤鍥犳灉鎺╃爜
            mask = torch.triu(
                torch.ones(i_end - i, seq, dtype=torch.bool, device=q.device),
                diagonal=seq - i
            )
            scores = scores.masked_fill(mask, float('-inf'))
            
            # Softmax鍜屽姞鏉?            attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            output[:, :, i:i_end, :] = torch.matmul(attn, v)
        
        return output
```

---

## 4. 鏁版嵁澶勭悊绯荤粺

鏁版嵁澶勭悊鏄缁冨ぇ璇█妯″瀷鐨勫叧閿幆鑺傦紝MiniMind鎻愪緵浜嗗畬鏁寸殑鏁版嵁澶勭悊娴佺▼銆傛暟鎹川閲忕洿鎺ュ喅瀹氫簡妯″瀷鐨勪笂闄愶紝鍥犳鐞嗚В鏁版嵁澶勭悊绯荤粺鑷冲叧閲嶈銆?
### 4.0 鏁版嵁澶勭悊绯荤粺鏋舵瀯鎬昏

```
鏁版嵁澶勭悊娴佺▼锛?
鍘熷鏁版嵁锛堟枃鏈?瀵硅瘽锛?       鈹?       鈻?鈹屸攢鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹?鈹?                   鏁版嵁娓呮礂涓庨澶勭悊                           鈹?鈹?                   鍒嗚瘝鍣ㄥ鐞?                                鈹?鈹? - 鍔犺浇棰勮缁冨垎璇嶅櫒                                          鈹?鈹? - 鏂囨湰 鈫?Token ID搴忓垪                                       鈹?鈹? - 娣诲姞鐗规畩Token锛圔OS/EOS/PAD锛?                             鈹?鈹斺攢鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹?       鈹?       鈻?鈹屸攢鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹?鈹?                   搴忓垪澶勭悊                                   鈹?鈹? - 鎴柇锛堣秴杩噈ax_length锛?                                   鈹?鈹? - 濉厖锛堜笉瓒砿ax_length锛?                                   鈹?鈹? - 鍒涘缓娉ㄦ剰鍔涙帺鐮?                                           鈹?鈹? - 鍒涘缓鏍囩锛堢敤浜庤缁冿級                                       鈹?鈹斺攢鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹?       鈹?       鈻?鈹屸攢鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹?鈹?                   鎵归噺缁勭粐                                   鈹?鈹? - DataLoader鎵瑰鐞?                                         鈹?鈹? - 鍒嗗竷寮忛噰鏍凤紙DDP锛?                                         鈹?鈹? - 鍔ㄦ€佹壒澶勭悊锛堝彲閫夛級                                         鈹?鈹斺攢鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹?       鈹?       鈻?    妯″瀷杈撳叆
```

### 4.0.1 鏁版嵁鏍煎紡瀵规瘮

| 鏁版嵁绫诲瀷 | 鏂囦欢鏍煎紡 | 涓昏鐢ㄩ€?| 鐗规畩澶勭悊 |
|---------|---------|---------|---------|
| 棰勮缁冩暟鎹?| .jsonl | 瀛︿範璇█鐭ヨ瘑 | 绾枃鏈紝鏃犺鑹插尯鍒?|
| SFT鏁版嵁 | .jsonl | 瀛︿範瀵硅瘽鑳藉姏 | 澶氳疆瀵硅瘽锛岃鑹插尯鍒?|
| DPO鏁版嵁 | .jsonl | 瀛︿範浜虹被鍋忓ソ | 姝ｈ礋鏍锋湰瀵规瘮 |

### 4.1 棰勮缁冩暟鎹泦

```python
"""
棰勮缁冩暟鎹泦澶勭悊
浣嶇疆锛歞ataset/lm_dataset.py
"""

import torch
from torch.utils.data import Dataset
import json
from typing import Optional


class PretrainDataset(Dataset):
    """
    棰勮缁冩暟鎹泦
    
    鐢ㄤ簬鍔犺浇鍜屽鐞嗛璁粌璇枡銆?    鏁版嵁鏍煎紡锛氭瘡琛屼竴涓狫SON瀵硅薄锛屽寘鍚?text"瀛楁
    
    绀轰緥鏁版嵁鏍煎紡锛?    {"text": "杩欐槸涓€娈甸璁粌鏂囨湰..."}
    {"text": "鍙︿竴娈甸璁粌鏂囨湰..."}
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        data_path: str,
        tokenizer,
        max_length: int = 512,
        buffer_size: int = 10000
    ):
        """
        鍒濆鍖栭璁粌鏁版嵁闆?        
        鍙傛暟:
            data_path: 鏁版嵁鏂囦欢璺緞锛?jsonl鏍煎紡锛?            tokenizer: 鍒嗚瘝鍣?            max_length: 鏈€澶у簭鍒楅暱搴?            buffer_size: 缂撳啿鍖哄ぇ灏忥紙鐢ㄤ簬鍐呭瓨浼樺寲锛?        """
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.max_length = max_length
        self.buffer_size = buffer_size
        
        # 鍔犺浇鏁版嵁
        self.data = []
        with open(data_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            for line in f:
                item = json.loads(line.strip())
                self.data.append(item['text'])
        
        print(f"鍔犺浇浜?{len(self.data)} 鏉￠璁粌鏁版嵁")
    
    def __len__(self):
        """杩斿洖鏁版嵁闆嗗ぇ灏?""
        return len(self.data)
    
    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> dict:
        """
        鑾峰彇鍗曚釜鏍锋湰
        
        鍙傛暟:
            idx: 鏍锋湰绱㈠紩
            
        杩斿洖:
            鍖呭惈input_ids鍜宭abels鐨勫瓧鍏?            
        澶勭悊娴佺▼:
            1. 鑾峰彇鏂囨湰
            2. Tokenize
            3. 鎴柇鍒版渶澶ч暱搴?            4. 鍒涘缓labels锛堜笌input_ids鐩稿悓锛岀敤浜庤瑷€妯″瀷璁粌锛?        """
        # 鑾峰彇鏂囨湰
        text = self.data[idx]
        
        # Tokenize
        # add_special_tokens=True: 娣诲姞BOS鍜孍OS token
        encoding = self.tokenizer(
            text,
            max_length=self.max_length,
            truncation=True,
            padding='max_length',
            return_tensors='pt',
            add_special_tokens=True
        )
        
        input_ids = encoding['input_ids'].squeeze(0)
        
        # 瀵逛簬璇█妯″瀷锛宭abels灏辨槸input_ids
        # 妯″瀷浼氶娴嬩笅涓€涓猼oken锛屾墍浠abels鏄痠nput_ids鐨勭Щ浣嶇増鏈?        # 浣嗗湪PyTorch涓紝浜ゅ弶鐔垫崯澶卞嚱鏁颁細鑷姩澶勭悊杩欎釜
        labels = input_ids.clone()
        
        # 灏唒adding token鐨刲abels璁句负-100锛岃繖鏍锋崯澶卞嚱鏁颁細蹇界暐瀹冧滑
        labels[labels == self.tokenizer.pad_token_id] = -100
        
        return {
            'input_ids': input_ids,
            'labels': labels
        }


class SFTDataset(Dataset):
    """
    鐩戠潱寰皟鏁版嵁闆?    
    鐢ㄤ簬鍔犺浇鍜屽鐞嗗璇濇暟鎹€?    鏁版嵁鏍煎紡锛氭瘡琛屼竴涓狫SON瀵硅薄锛屽寘鍚?conversations"瀛楁
    
    绀轰緥鏁版嵁鏍煎紡锛?    {
        "conversations": [
            {"role": "user", "content": "浣犲ソ"},
            {"role": "assistant", "content": "浣犲ソ锛佹湁浠€涔堟垜鍙互甯姪浣犵殑鍚楋紵"}
        ]
    }
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        data_path: str,
        tokenizer,
        max_length: int = 512
    ):
        """
        鍒濆鍖朣FT鏁版嵁闆?        
        鍙傛暟:
            data_path: 鏁版嵁鏂囦欢璺緞
            tokenizer: 鍒嗚瘝鍣?            max_length: 鏈€澶у簭鍒楅暱搴?        """
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.max_length = max_length
        
        # 鍔犺浇鏁版嵁
        self.data = []
        with open(data_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            for line in f:
                item = json.loads(line.strip())
                self.data.append(item)
        
        print(f"鍔犺浇浜?{len(self.data)} 鏉FT鏁版嵁")
    
    def _create_chat_prompt(self, conversations: list) -> str:
        """
        鍒涘缓瀵硅瘽鎻愮ず
        
        浣跨敤ChatML鏍煎紡锛?        <|im_start|>user
        鐢ㄦ埛娑堟伅<|im_end|>
        <|im_start|>assistant
        鍔╂墜鍥炲<|im_end|>
        
        鍙傛暟:
            conversations: 瀵硅瘽鍒楄〃
            
        杩斿洖:
            鏍煎紡鍖栧悗鐨勫璇濆瓧绗︿覆
        """
        prompt = ""
        for conv in conversations:
            role = conv['role']
            content = conv['content']
            
            if role == 'user':
                prompt += f"<|im_start|>user\n{content}<|im_end|>\n"
            elif role == 'assistant':
                prompt += f"<|im_start|>assistant\n{content}<|im_end|>\n"
        
        return prompt
    
    def __len__(self):
        """杩斿洖鏁版嵁闆嗗ぇ灏?""
        return len(self.data)
    
    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> dict:
        """
        鑾峰彇鍗曚釜鏍锋湰
        
        鍙傛暟:
            idx: 鏍锋湰绱㈠紩
            
        杩斿洖:
            鍖呭惈input_ids鍜宭abels鐨勫瓧鍏?            
        澶勭悊娴佺▼:
            1. 鑾峰彇瀵硅瘽
            2. 鏍煎紡鍖栦负ChatML鏍煎紡
            3. Tokenize
            4. 鍒涘缓labels锛堝彧璁＄畻assistant鍥炲鐨勬崯澶憋級
        """
        # 鑾峰彇瀵硅瘽
        conversations = self.data[idx]['conversations']
        
        # 鍒涘缓鎻愮ず
        prompt = self._create_chat_prompt(conversations)
        
        # Tokenize
        encoding = self.tokenizer(
            prompt,
            max_length=self.max_length,
            truncation=True,
            padding='max_length',
            return_tensors='pt'
        )
        
        input_ids = encoding['input_ids'].squeeze(0)
        
        # 鍒涘缓labels
        # 鍙绠梐ssistant鍥炲閮ㄥ垎鐨勬崯澶?        labels = input_ids.clone()
        
        # 鎵惧埌assistant鍥炲鐨勪綅缃?        # 灏唘ser閮ㄥ垎鍜宲adding閮ㄥ垎鐨刲abels璁句负-100
        # 杩欐牱鎹熷け鍑芥暟鍙細璁＄畻assistant鍥炲閮ㄥ垎
        
        # 绠€鍖栧鐞嗭細杩欓噷鎴戜滑璁＄畻鎵€鏈塼oken鐨勬崯澶?        # 瀹為檯搴旂敤涓簲璇ュ彧璁＄畻assistant閮ㄥ垎
        labels[labels == self.tokenizer.pad_token_id] = -100
        
        return {
            'input_ids': input_ids,
            'labels': labels
        }
```

璇█寤烘ā鐩爣锛氶娴嬩笅涓€涓猼oken

缁欏畾鏂囨湰搴忓垪 x = [x鈧? x鈧? ..., x鈧橾
鐩爣锛氭渶澶у寲 P(x鈧? x鈧? ..., x鈧?

鏍规嵁閾惧紡娉曞垯锛?    P(x鈧? x鈧? ..., x鈧? = P(x鈧? 脳 P(x鈧倈x鈧? 脳 P(x鈧億x鈧?x鈧? 脳 ... 脳 P(x鈧檤x鈧?...,x鈧欌倠鈧?

璁粌鐩爣锛?    L = -危 log P(x岬?| x鈧? ..., x岬⑩倠鈧?
    
杩欏氨鏄?涓嬩竴涓猼oken棰勬祴"浠诲姟锛屼篃绉颁负"鍥犳灉璇█寤烘ā"锛圕ausal Language Modeling锛?```

#### 5.0.3 棰勮缁冩暟鎹妯″缓璁?
| 妯″瀷鍙傛暟閲?| 寤鸿鏁版嵁閲?| 鏁版嵁/鍙傛暟姣?|

**Chinchilla瀹氬緥**锛氭渶浼樿缁冩暟鎹噺绾︿负鍙傛暟閲忕殑20鍊?
### 5.1 棰勮缁冭剼鏈畬鏁磋В鏋?
```python
"""
棰勮缁冭剼鏈?浣嶇疆锛?-pretrain.py
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torch.utils.data.distributed import DistributedSampler
import torch.distributed as dist
from torch.nn.parallel import DistributedDataParallel as DDP
import argparse
import math
import os
from contextlib import nullcontext

# 瀵煎叆妯″瀷鍜屾暟鎹泦
from model.model import MiniMindLM
from model.LMConfig import LMConfig
from dataset.lm_dataset import PretrainDataset
from transformers import AutoTokenizer


def get_lr(current_step: int, total_steps: int, lr: float) -> float:
    """
    浣欏鸡瀛︿範鐜囪皟搴?    
    鍏紡锛?        lr = min_lr + 0.5 * (lr - min_lr) * (1 + cos(蟺 * step / total_steps))
    
    鍙傛暟:
        current_step: 褰撳墠姝ユ暟
        total_steps: 鎬绘鏁?        lr: 鍩虹瀛︿範鐜?        
    杩斿洖:
        褰撳墠瀛︿範鐜?        
    瀛︿範鐜囧彉鍖栨洸绾匡細
        寮€濮嬫椂锛歭r
        涓棿锛氶€愭笎涓嬮檷
        缁撴潫鏃讹細lr / 10
    """
    # 鏈€灏忓涔犵巼
    min_lr = lr / 10
    
    # 浣欏鸡閫€鐏?    return min_lr + 0.5 * (lr - min_lr) * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * current_step / total_steps))


def train(args):
    """
    涓昏缁冨嚱鏁?    
    鍙傛暟:
        args: 鍛戒护琛屽弬鏁?    """
    # ==================== 鍒濆鍖栧垎甯冨紡璁粌 ====================
    
    # 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚︿娇鐢ㄥ垎甯冨紡璁粌
    ddp = int(os.environ.get('RANK', -1)) != -1
    
    if ddp:
        # 鍒濆鍖栬繘绋嬬粍
        dist.init_process_group(backend='nccl')
        ddp_rank = int(os.environ['RANK'])
        ddp_local_rank = int(os.environ['LOCAL_RANK'])
        ddp_world_size = int(os.environ['WORLD_SIZE'])
        device = f'cuda:{ddp_local_rank}'
        torch.cuda.set_device(device)
        master_process = ddp_rank == 0
    else:
        ddp_rank = 0
        ddp_local_rank = 0
        ddp_world_size = 1
        device = args.device
        master_process = True
    
    # ==================== 璁剧疆闅忔満绉嶅瓙 ====================
    
    torch.manual_seed(42)
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.manual_seed(42)
    
    # ==================== 鍔犺浇鍒嗚瘝鍣?====================
    
    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('./model/minimind_tokenizer')
    print(f"璇嶆眹琛ㄥぇ灏? {len(tokenizer)}")
    
    # ==================== 鍒涘缓妯″瀷閰嶇疆 ====================
    
    lm_config = LMConfig(
        dim=args.dim,
        n_layers=args.n_layers,
        max_seq_len=args.max_seq_len,
        use_moe=args.use_moe
    )
    
    # ==================== 鍒涘缓妯″瀷 ====================
    
    model = MiniMindLM(lm_config).to(device)
    
    # 璁＄畻鍙傛暟閲?    total_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
    print(f"妯″瀷鍙傛暟閲? {total_params / 1e6:.2f}M")
    
    # 鍖呰DDP
    if ddp:
        model = DDP(model, device_ids=[ddp_local_rank])
    
    # ==================== 鍒涘缓鏁版嵁闆嗗拰鏁版嵁鍔犺浇鍣?====================
    
    train_dataset = PretrainDataset(
        args.data_path,
        tokenizer,
        max_length=lm_config.max_seq_len
    )
    
    # 鍒嗗竷寮忛噰鏍峰櫒
    train_sampler = DistributedSampler(train_dataset) if ddp else None
    
    train_loader = DataLoader(
        train_dataset,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
        pin_memory=True,
        drop_last=False,
        shuffle=False if ddp else True,
        num_workers=args.num_workers,
        sampler=train_sampler
    )
    
    # ==================== 鍒涘缓浼樺寲鍣?====================
    
    # AdamW浼樺寲鍣?    # weight_decay: 鏉冮噸琛板噺锛岄槻姝㈣繃鎷熷悎
    # betas: Adam鐨勫姩閲忓弬鏁?    optimizer = optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(),
        lr=args.learning_rate,
        betas=(0.9, 0.95),
        weight_decay=0.1
    )
    
    # ==================== 鍒涘缓姊害缂╂斁鍣?====================
    
    # 鐢ㄤ簬娣峰悎绮惧害璁粌
    # 娣峰悎绮惧害鍙互鍑忓皯鏄惧瓨鍗犵敤骞跺姞閫熻缁?    scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=(args.dtype == 'float16'))
    
    # ==================== 璁＄畻璁粌姝ユ暟 ====================
    
    total_steps = args.epochs * len(train_loader) // args.accumulation_steps
    
    # ==================== 璁粌寰幆 ====================
    
    model.train()
    
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        # 璁剧疆閲囨牱鍣╡poch
        if ddp:
            train_sampler.set_epoch(epoch)
        
        # 绱Н姊害璁℃暟
        accumulation_step = 0
        
        for step, batch in enumerate(train_loader):
            # 鑾峰彇杈撳叆
            input_ids = batch['input_ids'].to(device)
            labels = batch['labels'].to(device)
            
            # 娣峰悎绮惧害涓婁笅鏂?            with torch.cuda.amp.autocast(enabled=(args.dtype in ['float16', 'bfloat16'])):
                # 鍓嶅悜浼犳挱
                outputs = model(input_ids)
                logits = outputs.logits
                
                # 璁＄畻鎹熷け
                # 浜ゅ弶鐔垫崯澶?                # logits: (batch, seq_len, vocab_size)
                # labels: (batch, seq_len)
                loss = nn.functional.cross_entropy(
                    logits.view(-1, logits.size(-1)),
                    labels.view(-1),
                    ignore_index=-100
                )
                
                # 娣诲姞MoE杈呭姪鎹熷け
                if outputs.aux_loss is not None:
                    loss += outputs.aux_loss
                
                # 姊害绱Н缂╂斁
                loss = loss / args.accumulation_steps
            
            # 鍙嶅悜浼犳挱
            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
            
            # 姊害绱Н
            accumulation_step += 1
            
            if accumulation_step >= args.accumulation_steps:
                # 姊害瑁佸壀
                scaler.unscale_(optimizer)
                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), args.grad_clip)
                
                # 鏇存柊鍙傛暟
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()
                
                # 娓呴浂姊害
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                
                # 鏇存柊瀛︿範鐜?                current_step = epoch * len(train_loader) + step
                lr = get_lr(current_step, total_steps, args.learning_rate)
                for param_group in optimizer.param_groups:
                    param_group['lr'] = lr
                
                accumulation_step = 0
                
                # 鏃ュ織
                if master_process and step % args.log_interval == 0:
                    print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Step {step}, Loss: {loss.item():.4f}, LR: {lr:.6f}")
                
                # 淇濆瓨妫€鏌ョ偣
                if master_process and step % args.save_interval == 0:
                    checkpoint = {
                        'model': model.module.state_dict() if ddp else model.state_dict(),
                        'optimizer': optimizer.state_dict(),
                        'epoch': epoch,
                        'step': step
                    }
                    torch.save(checkpoint, f'{args.out_dir}/pretrain_{args.dim}.pth')
    
    # 淇濆瓨鏈€缁堟ā鍨?    if master_process:
        checkpoint = {
            'model': model.module.state_dict() if ddp else model.state_dict(),
            'optimizer': optimizer.state_dict(),
        }
        torch.save(checkpoint, f'{args.out_dir}/pretrain_{args.dim}.pth')
        print("璁粌瀹屾垚锛?)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="MiniMind Pretraining")
    
    # 璁粌鍙傛暟
    parser.add_argument("--out_dir", type=str, default="out")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--learning_rate", type=float, default=5e-4)
    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda:0")
    parser.add_argument("--dtype", type=str, default="bfloat16")
    parser.add_argument("--num_workers", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--accumulation_steps", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--grad_clip", type=float, default=1.0)
    parser.add_argument("--log_interval", type=int, default=100)
    parser.add_argument("--save_interval", type=int, default=100)
    
    # 妯″瀷鍙傛暟
    parser.add_argument("--dim", type=int, default=512)
    parser.add_argument("--n_layers", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--max_seq_len", type=int, default=512)
    parser.add_argument("--use_moe", action="store_true")
    
    # 鏁版嵁鍙傛暟
    parser.add_argument("--data_path", type=str, default="./dataset/pretrain_hq.jsonl")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # 鍒涘缓杈撳嚭鐩綍
    os.makedirs(args.out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    # 寮€濮嬭缁?    train(args)
```

鐩戠潱寰皟锛圫upervised Fine-Tuning锛夎妯″瀷瀛︿細閬靛惊鎸囦护鍜岃繘琛屽璇濄€係FT鏄皢棰勮缁冩ā鍨嬭浆鍖栦负瀵硅瘽鍔╂墜鐨勫叧閿楠ゃ€?
### 6.0 SFT鐞嗚鑳屾櫙

棰勮缁冩ā鍨嬬殑闂锛?1. 鍙細"缁啓"锛屼笉浼?鍥炵瓟"
   杈撳叆锛?浠€涔堟槸鏈哄櫒瀛︿範锛?
   棰勮缁冩ā鍨嬭緭鍑猴細"浠€涔堟槸娣卞害瀛︿範锛熶粈涔堟槸浜哄伐鏅鸿兘锛?锛堢户缁彁闂級
   
2. 涓嶇悊瑙ｆ寚浠ゆ牸寮?   鏃犳硶鍖哄垎鐢ㄦ埛杈撳叆鍜屽姪鎵嬪洖澶?

SFT鐨勪綔鐢細
1. 瀛︿範瀵硅瘽鏍煎紡锛圕hatML鏍煎紡锛?2. 瀛︿細鍥炵瓟鑰岄潪缁啓
3. 鍒濇瀵归綈浜虹被鎰忓浘
```

浣犳槸涓€涓湁甯姪鐨凙I鍔╂墜銆?|im_end|>
<|im_start|>user
浠€涔堟槸鏈哄櫒瀛︿範锛?|im_end|>
<|im_start|>assistant
鏈哄櫒瀛︿範鏄汉宸ユ櫤鑳界殑涓€涓垎鏀?..<|im_end|>

鏍煎紡璇存槑锛?- <|im_start|>role锛氳鑹插紑濮嬫爣璁?- <|im_end|>锛氳鑹茬粨鏉熸爣璁?- role鍙互鏄細system銆乽ser銆乤ssistant

涓轰粈涔堜娇鐢–hatML锛?1. 鏄庣‘鐨勮鑹茶竟鐣?2. 鏀寔澶氳疆瀵硅瘽
3. 鏄撲簬瑙ｆ瀽鍜屽鐞?4. 涓庝富娴佹ā鍨嬶紙Qwen銆丟LM绛夛級鍏煎

#### 6.0.3 SFT璁粌绛栫暐

```
SFT璁粌鐨勫叧閿缃細

1. 瀛︿範鐜?   - 棰勮缁冨涔犵巼锛?e-4
   - SFT瀛︿範鐜囷細1e-5 鍒?1e-4
   - 鍘熷洜锛氬井璋冮渶瑕佸皬骞呭害璋冩暣锛岄伩鍏嶇牬鍧忛璁粌鐭ヨ瘑

2. 璁粌杞暟
   - 棰勮缁冿細1-3杞?   - SFT锛?-10杞?   - 鍘熷洜锛歋FT鏁版嵁閲忓皬锛岄渶瑕佸杞涔?
3. 鎹熷け璁＄畻
   - 棰勮缁冿細璁＄畻鎵€鏈塼oken鐨勬崯澶?   - SFT锛氬彧璁＄畻assistant鍥炲鐨勬崯澶?   - 鍘熷洜锛氶伩鍏嶆ā鍨嬪涔犳ā浠跨敤鎴?
4. 鏁版嵁閰嶆瘮
   - 澶氫换鍔℃贩鍚堬細涓嶅悓绫诲瀷浠诲姟鐨勬暟鎹贩鍚?   - 鏁版嵁澧炲己锛氭敼鍐欍€佺炕璇戠瓑鏂瑰紡鎵╁厖鏁版嵁
   - 璐ㄩ噺浼樺厛锛氶珮璐ㄩ噺鏁版嵁姣斿ぇ閲忔暟鎹洿閲嶈
```

### 6.0.4 SFT鏁版嵁璐ㄩ噺鏍囧噯

| 缁村害 | 楂樿川閲忔爣鍑?| 浣庤川閲忕ず渚?|
|------|-----------|-----------|
| 鎸囦护娓呮櫚搴?| 鏄庣‘銆佸叿浣撱€佸彲鎵ц | 妯＄硦銆佸涔夈€佷笉瀹屾暣 |
| 鍥炲鐩稿叧鎬?| 鐩存帴鍥炵瓟闂 | 鍋忛銆佺瓟闈炴墍闂?|
| 鍥炲鍑嗙‘鎬?| 浜嬪疄姝ｇ‘銆侀€昏緫娓呮櫚 | 閿欒淇℃伅銆侀€昏緫娣蜂贡 |
| 鍥炲瀹屾暣鎬?| 鍏呭垎鍥炵瓟銆佹湁绀轰緥 | 杩囦簬绠€鐭€佷俊鎭笉瓒?|
| 璇█娴佺晠鎬?| 鑷劧銆佹祦鐣呫€佷笓涓?| 鐢熺‖銆佽娉曢敊璇?|

### 6.1 SFT寰皟鑴氭湰瀹屾暣瑙ｆ瀽
  
  ```python
"""
SFT寰皟鑴氭湰
浣嶇疆锛?-full_sft.py
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
import argparse
import os

from model.model import MiniMindLM
from model.LMConfig import LMConfig
from dataset.lm_dataset import SFTDataset
from transformers import AutoTokenizer


def train_sft(args):
    """
    SFT璁粌鍑芥暟
    
    SFT涓庨璁粌鐨勪富瑕佸尯鍒細
    1. 鏁版嵁鏍煎紡锛氬璇濇暟鎹€岄潪绾枃鏈?    2. 鎹熷け璁＄畻锛氬彧璁＄畻assistant鍥炲閮ㄥ垎
    3. 瀛︿範鐜囷細閫氬父鏇村皬
    4. 璁粌杞暟锛氶€氬父鏇村
    """
    # 璁惧
    device = args.device
    
    # 鍔犺浇鍒嗚瘝鍣?    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('./model/minimind_tokenizer')
    
    # 鍒涘缓妯″瀷閰嶇疆
    lm_config = LMConfig(
        dim=args.dim,
        n_layers=args.n_layers,
        max_seq_len=args.max_seq_len
    )
    
    # 鍒涘缓妯″瀷
    model = MiniMindLM(lm_config).to(device)
    
    # 鍔犺浇棰勮缁冩潈閲?    if args.pretrain_path:
        checkpoint = torch.load(args.pretrain_path, map_location=device)
        model.load_state_dict(checkpoint['model'])
        print(f"鍔犺浇棰勮缁冩潈閲? {args.pretrain_path}")
    
    # 鍒涘缓鏁版嵁闆?    train_dataset = SFTDataset(
        args.data_path,
        tokenizer,
        max_length=lm_config.max_seq_len
    )
    
    train_loader = DataLoader(
        train_dataset,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
        shuffle=True,
        num_workers=args.num_workers
    )
    
    # 浼樺寲鍣?    optimizer = optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(),
        lr=args.learning_rate,
        weight_decay=0.01
    )
    
    # 璁粌寰幆
    model.train()
    
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        for step, batch in enumerate(train_loader):
            input_ids = batch['input_ids'].to(device)
            labels = batch['labels'].to(device)
            
            # 鍓嶅悜浼犳挱
            outputs = model(input_ids)
            logits = outputs.logits
            
            # 璁＄畻鎹熷け
            loss = nn.functional.cross_entropy(
                logits.view(-1, logits.size(-1)),
                labels.view(-1),
                ignore_index=-100
            )
            
            # 鍙嶅悜浼犳挱
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            
            # 姊害瑁佸壀
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
            
            # 鏇存柊鍙傛暟
            optimizer.step()
            
            # 鏃ュ織
            if step % 10 == 0:
                print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Step {step}, Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")
    
    # 淇濆瓨妯″瀷
    torch.save(model.state_dict(), f'{args.out_dir}/full_sft_{args.dim}.pth')
    print("SFT璁粌瀹屾垚锛?)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="MiniMind SFT")
    parser.add_argument("--out_dir", type=str, default="out")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=10)
    parser.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--learning_rate", type=float, default=1e-5)
    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda:0")
    parser.add_argument("--dim", type=int, default=512)
    parser.add_argument("--n_layers", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--max_seq_len", type=int, default=512)
    parser.add_argument("--data_path", type=str, default="./dataset/sft_mini_512.jsonl")
    parser.add_argument("--pretrain_path", type=str, default="./out/pretrain_512.pth")
    parser.add_argument("--num_workers", type=int, default=1)
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    train_sft(args)
```

---

## 7. LoRA寰皟

LoRA锛圠ow-Rank Adaptation锛夋槸涓€绉嶅弬鏁伴珮鏁堢殑寰皟鏂规硶锛屽畠閫氳繃鍦ㄩ璁粌鏉冮噸鏃佽竟娣诲姞浣庣З鍒嗚В鐭╅樀鏉ュ疄鐜板井璋冿紝澶у箙鍑忓皯浜嗛渶瑕佽缁冪殑鍙傛暟閲忋€?
### 7.0 LoRA鐞嗚鑳屾櫙

#### 7.0.1 LoRA鐨勬牳蹇冩€濇兂

```
LoRA鐨勬牳蹇冨亣璁撅細
妯″瀷閫傚簲鏂颁换鍔℃椂锛屾潈閲嶆洿鏂扮殑鍐呭湪缁村害寰堜綆

鏁板琛ㄨ揪锛?鍋囪棰勮缁冩潈閲?W鈧€ 鈭?R^(d脳k)
寰皟鍚庣殑鏉冮噸 W = W鈧€ + 螖W
LoRA鍋囪 螖W 鍙互鍒嗚В涓轰綆绉╃煩闃碉細螖W = BA

鍏朵腑锛?- B 鈭?R^(d脳r)锛氶檷缁寸煩闃?- A 鈭?R^(r脳k)锛氬崌缁寸煩闃?- r << min(d, k)锛氱З

鍓嶅悜浼犳挱锛?h = W鈧€x + 螖Wx = W鈧€x + BAx

鍏抽敭浼樺娍锛?1. 鍙傛暟閲忓ぇ骞呭噺灏?   鍘熷锛歞 脳 k
   LoRA锛歞 脳 r + r 脳 k = r 脳 (d + k)
   
2. 璁粌鏁堢巼鎻愬崌
   鍙渶璁粌A鍜孊锛屽喕缁揥鈧€
   
3. 鏄撲簬鍒囨崲浠诲姟
   涓嶅悓浠诲姟鍙渶涓嶅悓鐨凩oRA鏉冮噸
```

#### 7.0.2 LoRA鍙傛暟璇﹁В

```
LoRA鍏抽敭鍙傛暟锛?
1. 绉╋紙rank锛?   - 鍐冲畾浣庣З鐭╅樀鐨勭淮搴?   - 甯哥敤鍊硷細4, 8, 16, 32, 64
   - 绉╄秺澶э紝琛ㄨ揪鑳藉姏瓒婂己锛屼絾鍙傛暟瓒婂
   
2. 缂╂斁鍥犲瓙锛坅lpha锛?   - 鎺у埗LoRA杈撳嚭鐨勫奖鍝嶇▼搴?   - 瀹為檯缂╂斁 = alpha / rank
   - 甯哥敤鍊硷細16, 32
   
3. 鐩爣妯″潡锛坱arget_modules锛?   - 鍐冲畾鍦ㄥ摢浜涘眰搴旂敤LoRA
   - 甯歌閫夋嫨锛?     * 娉ㄦ剰鍔涘眰锛歸q, wk, wv, wo
     * FFN灞傦細w1, w2, w3
     * 鍏ㄩ儴锛氭墍鏈夌嚎鎬у眰
   
4. Dropout
   - LoRA灞傚唴閮ㄧ殑dropout
   - 闃叉杩囨嫙鍚?   - 甯哥敤鍊硷細0.0 - 0.1
```

#### 7.0.3 LoRA鍙傛暟閲忚绠?
```
浠iniMind-26M涓轰緥锛?
鍘熷妯″瀷鍙傛暟锛?- dim = 512
- n_layers = 8
- 娉ㄦ剰鍔涘眰锛? 脳 512 脳 512 = 1,048,576 鍙傛暟/灞?- FFN灞傦細3 脳 512 脳 1408 = 2,158,592 鍙傛暟/灞?
LoRA閰嶇疆锛?- rank = 8
- alpha = 16
- target_modules = ['wq', 'wk', 'wv', 'wo', 'w1', 'w2', 'w3']

姣忓眰LoRA鍙傛暟锛?- wq: 512 脳 8 + 8 脳 512 = 8,192
- wk: 512 脳 8 + 8 脳 512 = 8,192
- wv: 512 脳 8 + 8 脳 512 = 8,192
- wo: 512 脳 8 + 8 脳 512 = 8,192
- w1: 512 脳 8 + 8 脳 1408 = 15,360
- w2: 1408 脳 8 + 8 脳 512 = 15,360
- w3: 512 脳 8 + 8 脳 1408 = 15,360

姣忓眰鎬昏锛?8,848 鍙傛暟
8灞傛€昏锛?30,784 鍙傛暟

鍙傛暟鍗犳瘮锛?30,784 / 26,000,000 鈮?2.4%

缁撹锛歀oRA鍙渶璁粌绾?.4%鐨勫弬鏁帮紒
```

#### 7.0.4 LoRA vs 鍏ㄥ弬鏁板井璋冨姣?
| 鐗规€?| 鍏ㄥ弬鏁板井璋?| LoRA寰皟 |
|------|-----------|---------|
| 鍙缁冨弬鏁伴噺 | 100% | 1-5% |
| 鏄惧瓨鍗犵敤锛堣缁冿級 | 楂?| 浣?|
| 璁粌閫熷害 | 鎱?| 蹇?|
| 妯″瀷鎬ц兘 | 鏈€浼?| 鎺ヨ繎鏈€浼?|
| 澶氫换鍔″垏鎹?| 闇€瑕佸畬鏁存潈閲?| 鍙渶鍒囨崲LoRA |
| 瀹炵幇澶嶆潅搴?| 绠€鍗?| 涓瓑 |
| 閫傜敤鍦烘櫙 | 璧勬簮鍏呰冻 | 璧勬簮鍙楅檺 |

### 7.1 LoRA寰皟鑴氭湰瀹屾暣瑙ｆ瀽
  
  ```python
"""
LoRA寰皟鑴氭湰
浣嶇疆锛?-lora_sft.py
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
import argparse
import os
import math

from model.model import MiniMindLM
from model.LMConfig import LMConfig
from dataset.lm_dataset import SFTDataset
from transformers import AutoTokenizer


class LoRALayer(nn.Module):
    """
    LoRA灞?    
    LoRA鐨勬牳蹇冩€濇兂锛氬湪棰勮缁冩潈閲嶆梺杈规坊鍔犱綆绉╁垎瑙ｇ煩闃?    
    鍏紡锛?        W' = W + BA
        鍏朵腑 B 鈭?R^(d脳r), A 鈭?R^(r脳k)
        r << min(d, k) 鏄З
    
    鍙傛暟閲忓姣旓細
        鍘熷锛歞 脳 k
        LoRA锛歞 脳 r + r 脳 k = r 脳 (d + k)
        
        褰?r << min(d, k) 鏃讹紝鍙傛暟閲忓ぇ骞呭噺灏?    
    渚嬪锛?        d = 512, k = 512, r = 8
        鍘熷锛?12 脳 512 = 262,144
        LoRA锛? 脳 (512 + 512) = 8,192
        鍑忓皯浜?97% 鐨勫弬鏁?    """
    
    def __init__(self, in_features: int, out_features: int, rank: int = 8, alpha: float = 16.0):
        """
        鍒濆鍖朙oRA灞?        
        鍙傛暟:
            in_features: 杈撳叆缁村害
            out_features: 杈撳嚭缁村害
            rank: LoRA绉?            alpha: 缂╂斁鍥犲瓙
        """
        super().__init__()
        
        self.rank = rank
        self.alpha = alpha
        self.scaling = alpha / rank
        
        # 浣庣З鐭╅樀
        # A: 闅忔満鍒濆鍖?        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(rank, in_features) * 0.01)
        # B: 鍒濆鍖栦负闆讹紝纭繚鍒濆鏃禠oRA涓嶈捣浣滅敤
        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_features, rank))
    
    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, original_output: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        鍓嶅悜浼犳挱
        
        鍙傛暟:
            x: 杈撳叆寮犻噺
            original_output: 鍘熷灞傜殑杈撳嚭
            
        杩斿洖:
            娣诲姞LoRA璋冩暣鍚庣殑杈撳嚭
        """
        # 璁＄畻 LoRA 杈撳嚭
        # x @ A^T @ B^T * scaling
        lora_output = (x @ self.lora_A.T @ self.lora_B.T) * self.scaling
        
        return original_output + lora_output


def apply_lora_to_model(model, rank=8, alpha=16.0, target_modules=['wq', 'wk', 'wv', 'wo', 'w1', 'w2', 'w3']):
    """
    灏哃oRA搴旂敤鍒版ā鍨?    
    鍙傛暟:
        model: 鍘熷妯″瀷
        rank: LoRA绉?        alpha: 缂╂斁鍥犲瓙
        target_modules: 瑕佸簲鐢↙oRA鐨勬ā鍧楀悕
        
    杩斿洖:
        搴旂敤浜哃oRA鐨勬ā鍨?    """
    for name, module in model.named_modules():
        # 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚︽槸鐩爣妯″潡
        for target in target_modules:
            if target in name and isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                # 鍒涘缓LoRA灞?                lora_layer = LoRALayer(
                    module.in_features,
                    module.out_features,
                    rank=rank,
                    alpha=alpha
                )
                
                # 灏哃oRA灞傛坊鍔犲埌妯″潡
                setattr(module, 'lora', lora_layer)
                
                # 鍐荤粨鍘熷鏉冮噸
                module.weight.requires_grad = False
    
    return model


def train_lora(args):
    """
    LoRA璁粌鍑芥暟
    """
    device = args.device
    
    # 鍔犺浇鍒嗚瘝鍣?    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('./model/minimind_tokenizer')
    
    # 鍒涘缓妯″瀷
    lm_config = LMConfig(dim=args.dim, n_layers=args.n_layers)
    model = MiniMindLM(lm_config).to(device)
    
    # 鍔犺浇棰勮缁冩潈閲?    checkpoint = torch.load(args.pretrain_path, map_location=device)
    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint['model'])
    
    # 搴旂敤LoRA
    model = apply_lora_to_model(model, rank=args.lora_rank, alpha=args.lora_alpha)
    
    # 鍙缁僉oRA鍙傛暟
    lora_params = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if 'lora' in n and p.requires_grad]
    
    print(f"LoRA鍙傛暟閲? {sum(p.numel() for p in lora_params) / 1e6:.2f}M")
    
    # 浼樺寲鍣?    optimizer = optim.AdamW(lora_params, lr=args.learning_rate)
    
    # 鏁版嵁闆?    train_dataset = SFTDataset(args.data_path, tokenizer, max_length=lm_config.max_seq_len)
    train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True)
    
    # 璁粌
    model.train()
    
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        for step, batch in enumerate(train_loader):
            input_ids = batch['input_ids'].to(device)
            labels = batch['labels'].to(device)
            
            # 鍓嶅悜浼犳挱
            outputs = model(input_ids)
            loss = nn.functional.cross_entropy(
                outputs.logits.view(-1, outputs.logits.size(-1)),
                labels.view(-1),
                ignore_index=-100
            )
            
            # 鍙嶅悜浼犳挱
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
            
            if step % 10 == 0:
                print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Step {step}, Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")
    
    # 淇濆瓨LoRA鏉冮噸
    lora_state_dict = {n: p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if 'lora' in n}
    torch.save(lora_state_dict, f'{args.out_dir}/lora_{args.dim}.pth')
    print("LoRA璁粌瀹屾垚锛?)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="MiniMind LoRA SFT")
    parser.add_argument("--dim", type=int, default=512)
    parser.add_argument("--n_layers", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--lora_rank", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--lora_alpha", type=float, default=16.0)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--learning_rate", type=float, default=1e-4)
    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda:0")
    parser.add_argument("--data_path", type=str, default="./dataset/sft_mini_512.jsonl")
    parser.add_argument("--pretrain_path", type=str, default="./out/pretrain_512.pth")
    parser.add_argument("--out_dir", type=str, default="out")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    train_lora(args)
```

## 8. DPO寮哄寲瀛︿範

DPO锛圖irect Preference Optimization锛夋槸涓€绉嶇畝鍗曢珮鏁堢殑寮哄寲瀛︿範鏂规硶銆?
```python
"""
DPO璁粌鑴氭湰
浣嶇疆锛?-dpo_train.py
"""

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset
import argparse
import json

from model.model import MiniMindLM
from model.LMConfig import LMConfig
from transformers import AutoTokenizer


class DPODataset(Dataset):
    """
    DPO鏁版嵁闆?    
    鏁版嵁鏍煎紡锛?    {
        "prompt": "鐢ㄦ埛闂",
        "chosen": "濂界殑鍥炵瓟",
        "rejected": "宸殑鍥炵瓟"
    }
    """
    
    def __init__(self, data_path: str, tokenizer, max_length: int = 512):
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.max_length = max_length
        
        self.data = []
        with open(data_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            for line in f:
                self.data.append(json.loads(line.strip()))
    
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.data)
    
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        item = self.data[idx]
        
        # 缂栫爜prompt + chosen
        chosen_encoding = self.tokenizer(
            item['prompt'] + item['chosen'],
            max_length=self.max_length,
            truncation=True,
            padding='max_length',
            return_tensors='pt'
        )
        
        # 缂栫爜prompt + rejected
        rejected_encoding = self.tokenizer(
            item['prompt'] + item['rejected'],
            max_length=self.max_length,
            truncation=True,
            padding='max_length',
            return_tensors='pt'
        )
        
        return {
            'chosen_input_ids': chosen_encoding['input_ids'].squeeze(0),
            'rejected_input_ids': rejected_encoding['input_ids'].squeeze(0)
        }


def dpo_loss(
    policy_chosen_logps: torch.Tensor,
    policy_rejected_logps: torch.Tensor,
    reference_chosen_logps: torch.Tensor,
    reference_rejected_logps: torch.Tensor,
    beta: float = 0.1
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    DPO鎹熷け鍑芥暟
    
    鍏紡锛?        L = -E[log 蟽(尾 * (log 蟺(y_chosen|x) - log 蟺(y_rejected|x) 
                          - log 蟺_ref(y_chosen|x) + log 蟺_ref(y_rejected|x)))]
    
    鍙傛暟:
        policy_chosen_logps: 绛栫暐妯″瀷瀵筩hosen鐨勫鏁版鐜?        policy_rejected_logps: 绛栫暐妯″瀷瀵箁ejected鐨勫鏁版鐜?        reference_chosen_logps: 鍙傝€冩ā鍨嬪chosen鐨勫鏁版鐜?        reference_rejected_logps: 鍙傝€冩ā鍨嬪rejected鐨勫鏁版鐜?        beta: KL鏁ｅ害鎯╃綒绯绘暟
        
    杩斿洖:
        DPO鎹熷け
    """
    # 璁＄畻瀵规暟姣旂巼
    chosen_logratios = policy_chosen_logps - reference_chosen_logps
    rejected_logratios = policy_rejected_logps - reference_rejected_logps
    
    # DPO鎹熷け
    loss = -F.logsigmoid(beta * (chosen_logratios - rejected_logratios)).mean()
    
    return loss


def get_logprobs(model, input_ids):
    """
    璁＄畻妯″瀷瀵硅緭鍏ョ殑瀵规暟姒傜巼
    
    鍙傛暟:
        model: 璇█妯″瀷
        input_ids: 杈撳叆token ID
        
    杩斿洖:
        瀵规暟姒傜巼
    """
    outputs = model(input_ids)
    logits = outputs.logits
    
    # 璁＄畻瀵规暟姒傜巼
    log_probs = F.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)
    
    # 鑾峰彇瀹為檯token鐨勫鏁版鐜?    token_log_probs = log_probs.gather(2, input_ids.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)
    
    # 姹傚拰寰楀埌搴忓垪瀵规暟姒傜巼
    return token_log_probs.sum(dim=-1)


def train_dpo(args):
    """
    DPO璁粌鍑芥暟
    """
    device = args.device
    
    # 鍔犺浇鍒嗚瘝鍣?    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('./model/minimind_tokenizer')
    
    # 鍒涘缓绛栫暐妯″瀷
    lm_config = LMConfig(dim=args.dim, n_layers=args.n_layers)
    policy_model = MiniMindLM(lm_config).to(device)
    
    # 鍔犺浇SFT鏉冮噸
    checkpoint = torch.load(args.sft_path, map_location=device)
    policy_model.load_state_dict(checkpoint)
    
    # 鍒涘缓鍙傝€冩ā鍨嬶紙鍐荤粨锛?    reference_model = MiniMindLM(lm_config).to(device)
    reference_model.load_state_dict(checkpoint)
    reference_model.eval()
    for param in reference_model.parameters():
        param.requires_grad = False
    
    # 鏁版嵁闆?    train_dataset = DPODataset(args.data_path, tokenizer, max_length=lm_config.max_seq_len)
    train_loader = DataLoader(train_dataset, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True)
    
    # 浼樺寲鍣?    optimizer = optim.AdamW(policy_model.parameters(), lr=args.learning_rate)
    
    # 璁粌
    policy_model.train()
    
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        for step, batch in enumerate(train_loader):
            chosen_input_ids = batch['chosen_input_ids'].to(device)
            rejected_input_ids = batch['rejected_input_ids'].to(device)
            
            # 璁＄畻绛栫暐妯″瀷鐨勫鏁版鐜?            policy_chosen_logps = get_logprobs(policy_model, chosen_input_ids)
            policy_rejected_logps = get_logprobs(policy_model, rejected_input_ids)
            
            # 璁＄畻鍙傝€冩ā鍨嬬殑瀵规暟姒傜巼
            with torch.no_grad():
                reference_chosen_logps = get_logprobs(reference_model, chosen_input_ids)
                reference_rejected_logps = get_logprobs(reference_model, rejected_input_ids)
            
            # 璁＄畻DPO鎹熷け
            loss = dpo_loss(
                policy_chosen_logps,
                policy_rejected_logps,
                reference_chosen_logps,
                reference_rejected_logps,
                beta=args.beta
            )
            
            # 鍙嶅悜浼犳挱
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
            
            if step % 10 == 0:
                print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Step {step}, Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")
    
    # 淇濆瓨妯″瀷
    torch.save(policy_model.state_dict(), f'{args.out_dir}/dpo_{args.dim}.pth')
    print("DPO璁粌瀹屾垚锛?)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="MiniMind DPO")
    parser.add_argument("--dim", type=int, default=512)
    parser.add_argument("--n_layers", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument("--learning_rate", type=float, default=1e-6)
    parser.add_argument("--beta", type=float, default=0.1)
    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda:0")
    parser.add_argument("--data_path", type=str, default="./dataset/dpo_data.jsonl")
    parser.add_argument("--sft_path", type=str, default="./out/full_sft_512.pth")
    parser.add_argument("--out_dir", type=str, default="out")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    train_dpo(args)
```

## 9. 妯″瀷鎺ㄧ悊

```python
"""
妯″瀷鎺ㄧ悊鑴氭湰
浣嶇疆锛?-eval.py
"""

import torch
import argparse
from transformers import AutoTokenizer

from model.model import MiniMindLM
from model.LMConfig import LMConfig


def generate(
    model,
    tokenizer,
    prompt: str,
    max_new_tokens: int = 100,
    temperature: float = 0.7,
    top_k: int = 50,
    top_p: float = 0.9,
    device: str = "cuda:0"
):
    """
    鏂囨湰鐢熸垚鍑芥暟
    
    鍙傛暟:
        model: 璇█妯″瀷
        tokenizer: 鍒嗚瘝鍣?        prompt: 杈撳叆鎻愮ず
        max_new_tokens: 鏈€澶х敓鎴恡oken鏁?        temperature: 娓╁害鍙傛暟
        top_k: top-k閲囨牱
        top_p: nucleus閲囨牱
        device: 璁惧
        
    杩斿洖:
        鐢熸垚鐨勬枃鏈?    """
    model.eval()
    
    # 缂栫爜杈撳叆
    input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors='pt').to(device)
    
    # 鐢熸垚
    with torch.no_grad():
        output_ids = model.generate(
            input_ids,
            max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
            temperature=temperature,
            top_k=top_k,
            top_p=top_p
        )
    
    # 瑙ｇ爜
    output_text = tokenizer.decode(output_ids[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
    
    return output_text


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="MiniMind Inference")
    parser.add_argument("--weight", type=str, default="full_sft")
    parser.add_argument("--dim", type=int, default=512)
    parser.add_argument("--n_layers", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda:0")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # 鍔犺浇鍒嗚瘝鍣?    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('./model/minimind_tokenizer')
    
    # 鍒涘缓妯″瀷
    lm_config = LMConfig(dim=args.dim, n_layers=args.n_layers)
    model = MiniMindLM(lm_config).to(args.device)
    
    # 鍔犺浇鏉冮噸
    weight_path = f'./out/{args.weight}_{args.dim}.pth'
    checkpoint = torch.load(weight_path, map_location=args.device)
    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint['model'] if 'model' in checkpoint else checkpoint)
    
    print(f"鍔犺浇妯″瀷: {weight_path}")
    print("杈撳叆 'quit' 閫€鍑?)
    
    # 浜や簰寮忓璇?    while True:
        prompt = input("\n鐢ㄦ埛: ")
        if prompt.lower() == 'quit':
            break
        
        # 鏍煎紡鍖栬緭鍏?        formatted_prompt = f"<|im_start|>user\n{prompt}<|im_end|>\n<|im_start|>assistant\n"
        
        # 鐢熸垚鍥炲
        response = generate(model, tokenizer, formatted_prompt, device=args.device)
        
        # 鎻愬彇鍔╂墜鍥炲
        if '<|im_start|>assistant' in response:
            response = response.split('<|im_start|>assistant')[-1]
        if '<|im_end|>' in response:
            response = response.split('<|im_end|>')[0]
        
        print(f"鍔╂墜: {response.strip()}")


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

## 11. 浼樺寲鍣ㄤ笌瀛︿範鐜囪皟搴?
### 11.1 AdamW浼樺寲鍣ㄨ瑙?
AdamW锛圓dam with Weight Decay锛夋槸璁粌澶фā鍨嬬殑鏍囬厤浼樺寲鍣ㄣ€?
```python
"""
AdamW绠楁硶璇﹁В

AdamW鏄疉dam鐨勫彉浣擄紝鍖哄埆鍦ㄤ簬鏉冮噸琛板噺鐨勬柟寮忋€?
Adam鐨勬潈閲嶈“鍑?
    胃 = 胃 - 伪 * (grad / (鈭歷 + 蔚)) - 伪 * 位 * 胃

AdamW鐨勬潈閲嶈“鍑?
    胃 = 胃 - 伪 * (grad / (鈭歷 + 蔚)) - 伪 * 位 * 胃
                                鈫?                    杩欓噷AdamW鐨勮“鍑忔槸鐙珛鐨?
鏁板鍏紡:
    m_t = 尾鈧?* m_{t-1} + (1 - 尾鈧? * g_t        # 姊害鐨勪竴闃剁煩浼拌锛堝姩閲忥級
    v_t = 尾鈧?* v_{t-1} + (1 - 尾鈧? * g_t虏      # 姊害鐨勪竴闃剁煩浼拌锛堟柟宸級
    m_hat = m_t / (1 - 尾鈧乛t)                    # 鍋忓樊鏍℃
    v_hat = v_t / (1 - 尾鈧俕t)                    # 鍋忓樊鏍℃
    胃 = 胃 - 伪 * m_hat / (鈭歷_hat + 蔚) - 伪 * 位 * 胃  # 鍙傛暟鏇存柊

MiniMind閰嶇疆:
    lr = 5e-4          # 瀛︿範鐜?    尾鈧?= 0.9           # 鍔ㄩ噺
    尾鈧?= 0.95          # 鏂瑰樊琛板噺
    weight_decay = 0.1 # 鏉冮噸琛板噺
"""
```

AdamW vs SGD锛?- AdamW鏀舵暃鏇村揩
- SGD娉涘寲鎬ц兘鍙兘鏇村ソ
- 灏忔ā鍨嬭缁冨父鐢ˋdamW
- 澶фā鍨嬪父鐢ˋdamW + 姊害瑁佸壀

### 11.2 瀛︿範鐜囪皟搴︾瓥鐣?
```python
"""
MiniMind浣跨敤鐨勫涔犵巼璋冨害锛氫綑寮﹂€€鐏?
鍏紡锛?    lr(t) = lr_min + 0.5 * (lr_max - lr_min) * (1 + cos(蟺 * t / T))

鍏朵腑锛?    lr(t): 鏃堕棿t鐨勫涔犵巼
    lr_min: 鏈€灏忓涔犵巼锛堥€氬父涓簂r_max鐨?/10锛?    lr_max: 鏈€澶у涔犵巼
    t: 褰撳墠姝ユ暟
    T: 鎬绘鏁?
璋冨害鏇茬嚎锛?    lr
    ^
    |鈥锯€锯€锯€锯€锯€锯€锯€锯€锯€?    |           鈥锯€锯€锯€锯€锯€?    |                 鈥锯€锯€?    |                     鈥?    |                       鈥锯€?    |                            鈥锯€锯€?    +---------------------------> t
"""

def get_cosine_lr(current_step, total_steps, lr_max, lr_min=None):
    """
    璁＄畻浣欏鸡閫€鐏涔犵巼
    
    鍙傛暟:
        current_step: 褰撳墠姝ユ暟
        total_steps: 鎬绘鏁?        lr_max: 鏈€澶у涔犵巼
        lr_min: 鏈€灏忓涔犵巼锛岄粯璁や负lr_max/10
    """
    if lr_min is None:
        lr_min = lr_max / 10
    
    progress = current_step / total_steps
    return lr_min + 0.5 * (lr_max - lr_min) * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))

# 瀛︿範鐜囬鐑紙Warmup锛?def get_warmup_cosine_lr(current_step, warmup_steps, total_steps, lr_max):
    """
    甯﹂鐑殑浣欏鸡閫€鐏涔犵巼
    
    鍓峸armup_steps姝ョ嚎鎬у鍔犲涔犵巼
    涔嬪悗浣跨敤浣欏鸡閫€鐏?    """
    if current_step < warmup_steps:
        # 绾挎€ч鐑?        return lr_max * current_step / warmup_steps
    else:
        # 浣欏鸡閫€鐏?        progress = (current_step - warmup_steps) / (total_steps - warmup_steps)
        return lr_max * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))
```

### 11.3 姊害瑁佸壀涓庢搴︾疮绉?
```python
"""
姊害瑁佸壀锛圙radient Clipping锛?
鐩殑锛氶槻姝㈡搴︾垎鐐?
鍏紡锛?    if ||g|| > threshold:
        g = g * threshold / ||g||

MiniMind閰嶇疆锛?    grad_clip = 1.0  # 瑁佸壀闃堝€?"""

# PyTorch瀹炵幇
torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)

"""
姊害绱Н锛圙radient Accumulation锛?
鐩殑锛氬湪鏄惧瓨鏈夐檺鐨勬儏鍐典笅妯℃嫙澶atch璁粌

鍘熺悊锛?    姝ｅ父: batch_size=32, 鐩存帴鏇存柊
    绱Н: batch_size=8, 绱Н4娆″悗鏇存柊
    
    effective_batch_size = batch_size * accumulation_steps
"""

# 姊害绱Н绀轰緥
accumulation_steps = 4
batch_size = 8
effective_batch_size = batch_size * accumulation_steps  # 32

for step, batch in enumerate(dataloader):
    # 鍓嶅悜浼犳挱
    loss = model(batch)
    loss = loss / accumulation_steps  # 缂╂斁鎹熷け
    loss.backward()  # 鍙嶅悜浼犳挱锛岀疮绉搴?    
    if (step + 1) % accumulation_steps == 0:
        # 绱Н浜?涓猙atch鐨勬搴﹀悗鏇存柊
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        optimizer.zero_grad()
```

## 12. 娣峰悎绮惧害璁粌

### 12.1 涓轰粈涔堥渶瑕佹贩鍚堢簿搴︼紵

```
绮惧害绫诲瀷瀵规瘮锛?
float32 (鍗曠簿搴?:
    - 32浣嶆诞鐐规暟
    - 绮惧害鏈€楂橈紝鏄惧瓨鍗犵敤澶?    - 璁粌鍜屾帹鐞嗛兘鍙敤

float16 (鍗婄簿搴?:
    - 16浣嶆诞鐐规暟
    - 绮惧害杈冧綆锛屾樉瀛樺崰鐢ㄥ皬
    - 鍙兘鍑虹幇鏁板€兼孩鍑?
bfloat16 (BF16):
    - 16浣嶆诞鐐规暟
    - 涓巉loat32鐩稿悓鐨勬寚鏁拌寖鍥?    - 绮惧害浠嬩簬float32鍜宖loat16涔嬮棿
    - 澶фā鍨嬭缁冩帹鑽愪娇鐢?
鏄惧瓨鍗犵敤瀵规瘮锛堜互26M鍙傛暟涓轰緥锛夛細
    float32: 26M * 4 bytes = 104 MB
    float16: 26M * 2 bytes = 52 MB
    bf16:    26M * 2 bytes = 52 MB
    
    姊害锛堜笌鏉冮噸鍚屽昂瀵革級:
    浼樺寲鍣ㄧ姸鎬侊紙AdamW闇€瑕?鍊嶆潈閲嶅ぇ灏忥級:
    
    鎬昏:
    float32: 104 + 104 + 208 = 416 MB
    bf16:    52 + 52 + 104 = 208 MB (鍑忓皯50%)
```

### 12.2 PyTorch娣峰悎绮惧害瀹炵幇

```python
"""
PyTorch娣峰悎绮惧害璁粌绀轰緥
"""

from torch.cuda.amp import autocast, GradScaler

# 鍒涘缓姊害缂╂斁鍣?scaler = GradScaler()

model = model.to('cuda')
model.train()

for batch in dataloader:
    input_ids = batch['input_ids'].to('cuda')
    labels = batch['labels'].to('cuda')
    
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    
    # 鍓嶅悜浼犳挱 - 鑷姩浣跨敤鍗婄簿搴?    with autocast(dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        outputs = model(input_ids)
        loss = F.cross_entropy(outputs.logits.view(-1, outputs.logits.size(-1)), labels)
    
    # 鍙嶅悜浼犳挱 - 鎹熷け闇€瑕佺缉鏀?    scaler.scale(loss).backward()
    
    # 姊害瑁佸壀锛堥渶瑕佸厛unscale锛?    scaler.unscale_(optimizer)
    torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
    
    # 鍙傛暟鏇存柊 - 鑷姩澶勭悊缂╂斁
    scaler.step(optimizer)
    scaler.update()
```

### 12.3 Flash Attention

```python
"""
Flash Attention璇﹁В

鏍囧噯娉ㄦ剰鍔涚殑闂锛?- 闇€瑕丱(n虏)鐨勬樉瀛樺瓨鍌ㄦ敞鎰忓姏鍒嗘暟鐭╅樀
- 澶ч噺HBM锛堥珮甯﹀鍐呭瓨锛夎闂?- 璁粌闀垮簭鍒楁椂鏄惧瓨鐖嗙偢

Flash Attention鐨勮В鍐虫柟妗堬細
- 鍒嗗潡璁＄畻锛岄伩鍏嶅瓨鍌ㄥ畬鏁存敞鎰忓姏鐭╅樀
- IO鎰熺煡璁捐锛屽噺灏慔BM璁块棶
- 鏄惧瓨浠嶰(n虏)闄嶅埌O(n)

鍏紡锛?    浣跨敤鍒嗗潡褰掍竴鍖栵細
    softmax(X) = normalize(exp(X - max(X)))
    
    鍒嗗潡璁＄畻鏃堕渶瑕侊細
    1. 鎸夊潡璁＄畻灞€閮╯oftmax
    2. 姝ｇ‘澶勭悊鏁板€肩ǔ瀹氭€?    3. 鍚堝苟寰楀埌鍏ㄥ眬缁撴灉

PyTorch浣跨敤锛?    F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
        q, k, v,
        attn_mask=None,
        dropout_p=0.0,
        is_causal=True  # 鑷姩鐢熸垚causal mask
    )
"""

# MiniMind涓殑Flash Attention閰嶇疆
config = LMConfig(flash_attn=True)  # 榛樿鍚敤

# 鏉′欢鍒ゆ柇
if config.flash_attn and hasattr(F, 'scaled_dot_product_attention'):
    # 浣跨敤Flash Attention
    output = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(xq, xk, xv, is_causal=True)
else:
    # 鍥為€€鍒版爣鍑嗗疄鐜?    output = standard_attention(xq, xk, xv)
```

## 13. 鍒嗗竷寮忚缁?
### 13.1 DDP锛圖istributed Data Parallel锛?
```python
"""
DDP鍒嗗竷寮忔暟鎹苟琛岃缁?
宸ヤ綔鍘熺悊锛?1. 姣忎釜杩涚▼鏈夊畬鏁寸殑妯″瀷鍓湰
2. 鏁版嵁鍦ㄤ笉鍚岃繘绋嬮棿鍒嗗壊
3. 姣忎釜杩涚▼鐙珛鍓嶅悜浼犳挱鍜屽弽鍚戜紶鎾?4. 姊害閫氳繃AllReduce鍚屾
5. 鎵€鏈夎繘绋嬩互鐩稿悓鐨勫弬鏁版洿鏂?
浼樺娍锛?- 绾挎€у姞閫熸瘮锛堟帴杩戠悊鎯冲€硷級
- 閫氫俊寮€閿€灏?- 瀹归敊鎬уソ
"""

# 鍒濆鍖?import torch.distributed as dist

dist.init_process_group(backend='nccl')  # NCCL鐢ㄤ簬GPU閫氫俊
local_rank = int(os.environ['LOCAL_RANK'])
torch.cuda.set_device(local_rank)

# 鍖呰妯″瀷
model = model.to(local_rank)
model = DDP(model, device_ids=[local_rank])

# 鏁版嵁鍔犺浇
train_sampler = DistributedSampler(dataset)
dataloader = DataLoader(dataset, sampler=train_sampler)

# 璁粌寰幆
for epoch in range(num_epochs):
    train_sampler.set_epoch(epoch)  # 鎵撲贡鏁版嵁
    for batch in dataloader:
        # 鏁版嵁绉诲埌褰撳墠GPU
        batch = batch.to(local_rank)
        
        outputs = model(batch)
        loss = outputs.loss / gradient_accumulation_steps
        loss.backward()
        
        # 浼樺寲鍣ㄦ洿鏂?        optimizer.step()
        optimizer.zero_grad()
```

ZeRO鏈変笁涓樁娈碉細

ZeRO-1:
    - 浼樺寲鍣ㄧ姸鎬佸垎鐗?    - 鍑忓皯绾?鍊嶆樉瀛?
ZeRO-2:

ZeRO-3:
    - 浼樺寲鍣ㄧ姸鎬?+ 姊害 + 鍙傛暟鍒嗙墖
    - 鍑忓皯绾鍊嶆樉瀛橈紙N=GPU鏁伴噺锛?
閰嶇疆绀轰緥锛?{
    "train_batch_size": 32,
    "gradient_accumulation_steps": 4,
    "fp16": {"enabled": True},
    "zero_optimization": {
        "stage": 2,
        "offload_optimizer": {"device": "cpu"}
    }
}
"""

# 浣跨敤DeepSpeed鍚姩
# deepspeed --num_gpus=4 train.py

## 14. 鎺ㄧ悊浼樺寲

### 14.1 KV缂撳瓨鏈哄埗

```python
"""
KV缂撳瓨锛圞ey-Value Cache锛?
鎺ㄧ悊鏃剁殑鐥涚偣锛?- 鐢熸垚绗琋涓猼oken鏃讹紝闇€瑕侀噸鏂拌绠楀墠N-1涓猼oken鐨勬敞鎰忓姏
- 鏃堕棿澶嶆潅搴(n虏)锛宯涓哄簭鍒楅暱搴?- 澶ч噺閲嶅璁＄畻

KV缂撳瓨鐨勮В鍐虫柟妗堬細
- 棣栨鍓嶅悜浼犳挱锛氳绠楀苟缂撳瓨鎵€鏈塊鍜孷
- 鍚庣画鍓嶅悜浼犳挱锛氬彧璁＄畻鏂皌oken鐨凨鍜孷锛屾嫾鎺ュ巻鍙茬紦瀛?
瀹炵幇锛?    # 棣栨鎺ㄧ悊
    outputs = model(input_ids)  # input_ids: [1, 10]
    past_kv = outputs.past_key_values
    
    # 澧為噺鎺ㄧ悊
    outputs = model(
        input_ids[:, -1:],      # 鍙緭鍏ユ柊token
        past_key_values=past_kv  # 浼犲叆缂撳瓨
    )
    
    # 缂撳瓨浼氳嚜鍔ㄦ洿鏂帮細cat([past_k, new_k], dim=1)
"""

# MiniMind鐢熸垚鍑芥暟涓殑KV缂撳瓨浣跨敤
def generate(self, input_ids, max_new_tokens=100, ...):
    past_key_values = None
    
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = self.forward(
            input_ids if past_key_values is None else input_ids[:, -1:],
            past_key_values=past_key_values,
            use_cache=True
        )
        
        past_key_values = outputs.past_key_values
        logits = outputs.logits[:, -1, :]
        
        # ... 閲囨牱涓嬩竴涓猼oken
        next_token = ...
        
        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token], dim=-1)
    
    return input_ids
```

### 14.2 閲囨牱绛栫暐

```python
"""
鏂囨湰鐢熸垚鐨勯噰鏍风瓥鐣?
1. Greedy Search锛堣椽蹇冩悳绱級
   - 鎬绘槸閫夋嫨姒傜巼鏈€楂樼殑token
   - 缂虹偣锛氬鏄撻櫡鍏ラ噸澶嶅惊鐜?   - 鐢ㄩ€旓細纭畾鎬т换鍔?
2. Top-K Sampling
   - 浠庢鐜囨渶楂樼殑K涓猼oken涓殢鏈洪€夋嫨
   - 寮曞叆闅忔満鎬э紝澧炲姞澶氭牱鎬?   - K鐨勯€夋嫨寰堝叧閿細澶皬闄愬埗澶氭牱锛屽お澶у彲鑳介€夊埌浣庤川閲弔oken

3. Nucleus Sampling (Top-P)
   - 浠庣疮绉鐜囪揪鍒癙鐨勬渶灏弔oken闆嗗悎涓噰鏍?   - 鑷€傚簲鍦拌皟鏁村€欓€塼oken鏁伴噺
   - 閫氬父姣擳op-K鏇寸ǔ瀹?
4. Temperature Sampling
   - 鎺у埗姒傜巼鍒嗗竷鐨勫钩婊戝害
   - T>1锛氬垎甯冩洿骞冲潶锛屽鍔犲鏍锋€?   - T<1锛氬垎甯冩洿灏栭攼锛屽鍔犵‘瀹氭€?   - T=1锛氬師濮嬪垎甯?"""

def sample_with_temperature(logits, temperature=1.0):
    """搴旂敤娓╁害缂╂斁"""
    if temperature != 1.0:
        logits = logits / temperature
    return logits

def top_k_sample(probs, k=50):
    """Top-K閲囨牱"""
    top_k_probs, top_k_indices = torch.topk(probs, k)
    # 浠巘op-k涓寜姒傜巼閲囨牱
    sampled_idx = torch.multinomial(top_k_probs, 1)
    return top_k_indices[sampled_idx]

def nucleus_sample(probs, p=0.9):
    """Nucleus (Top-P) 閲囨牱"""
    sorted_probs, sorted_indices = torch.sort(probs, descending=True)
    cumulative_probs = torch.cumsum(sorted_probs, dim=-1)
    
    # 鎵惧埌绱Н姒傜巼瓒呰繃P鐨則oken
    nucleus_mask = cumulative_probs <= p
    # 纭繚鑷冲皯鍖呭惈涓€涓猼oken
    nucleus_mask[..., 1:] = nucleus_mask[..., :-1].clone()
    nucleus_mask[..., 0] = True
    
    # 杩囨护姒傜巼
    nucleus_probs = torch.where(nucleus_mask, sorted_probs, 0)
    nucleus_probs = nucleus_probs / nucleus_probs.sum()
    
    # 閲囨牱
    sampled_idx = torch.multinomial(nucleus_probs, 1)
    return sorted_indices[sampled_idx]
```

### 缁冧範2锛氬疄鐜拌嚜瀹氫箟閲囨牱

```python
"""
缁冧範锛氬疄鐜颁竴涓甫閲嶅鎯╃綒鐨勯噰鏍峰嚱鏁?
瑕佹眰锛?- 鍦ㄧ敓鎴愭椂鎯╃綒宸查€夋嫨杩囩殑token
- 鎯╃綒绯绘暟鍙皟
- 淇濇寔閲囨牱澶氭牱鎬?
鍏紡锛?    logits[token] = logits[token] / (1 + repeat_penalty * count[token])
"""

def sample_with_repetition_penalty(
    logits,
    previous_tokens,
    penalty=1.2
):
    """
    甯﹂噸澶嶆儵缃氱殑閲囨牱
    
    鍙傛暟:
        logits: 妯″瀷杈撳嚭鐨刲ogits
        previous_tokens: 涔嬪墠鐢熸垚鐨則oken搴忓垪
        penalty: 鎯╃綒绯绘暟锛?1澧炲姞鎯╃綒锛?1鍑忓皯鎯╃綒
    """
    # 缁熻token鍑虹幇娆℃暟
    token_counts = torch.bincount(previous_tokens, minlength=logits.size(-1))
    
    # 搴旂敤鎯╃綒锛氬凡鍑虹幇鐨則oken姒傜巼闄嶄綆
    penalties = 1.0 + (token_counts > 0).float() * (penalty - 1.0)
    logits = logits / penalties
    
    return logits
```

### 缁冧範3锛氬垎鏋愭敞鎰忓姏妯″紡

```python
"""
缁冧範锛氬彲瑙嗗寲妯″瀷鐨勬敞鎰忓姏鍒嗗竷

瑕佹眰锛?- 鎻愬彇鏌愪竴灞傜殑娉ㄦ剰鍔涙潈閲?- 鍙鍖栦笉鍚屼綅缃箣闂寸殑娉ㄦ剰鍔涘垎甯?- 鍒嗘瀽妯″瀷鍏虫敞鐨勪綅缃?
鎻愮ず锛?- 鍦╝ttention璁＄畻鏃朵繚瀛樻敞鎰忓姏鍒嗘暟
- 浣跨敤matplotlib鍙鍖?"""

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

def visualize_attention(attention_weights, title="Attention Pattern"):
    """
    鍙鍖栨敞鎰忓姏鏉冮噸
    
    attention_weights: (seq_len, seq_len) 鐨勬敞鎰忓姏鐭╅樀
    """
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 8))
    plt.imshow(attention_weights.cpu().numpy(), cmap='viridis')
    plt.colorbar()
    plt.title(title)
    plt.xlabel("Key Position")
    plt.ylabel("Query Position")
    plt.show()
```

## 鎬荤粨

閫氳繃杩欎唤璇﹀敖鐨勬暀绋嬶紝浣犲凡缁忎簡瑙ｄ簡MiniMind椤圭洰鐨勬瘡涓€涓牳蹇冪粍浠讹細

1. **閰嶇疆绯荤粺**锛氱悊瑙ｄ簡鎵€鏈夎秴鍙傛暟鐨勫惈涔夊拰浣滅敤
2. **RMSNorm**锛氭帉鎻′簡鐜颁唬LLM鐨勫綊涓€鍖栨柟娉?3. **RoPE**锛氱悊瑙ｄ簡鏃嬭浆浣嶇疆缂栫爜鐨勬暟瀛﹀師鐞?4. **GQA**锛氬浼氫簡鍒嗙粍鏌ヨ娉ㄦ剰鍔涚殑瀹炵幇
5. **SwiGLU**锛氫簡瑙ｄ簡闂ㄦ帶婵€娲诲嚱鏁扮殑浼樺娍
6. **MoE**锛氭帉鎻′簡娣峰悎涓撳绯荤粺鐨勮璁?7. **璁粌娴佺▼**锛氫粠棰勮缁冨埌DPO鐨勫畬鏁存祦绋?8. **Tokenizer**锛氱悊瑙ｄ簡鍒嗚瘝鍣ㄧ殑鍘熺悊鍜屽疄鐜?9. **浼樺寲鍣?*锛氭繁鍏ョ悊瑙ｄ簡AdamW鍜屽涔犵巼璋冨害
10. **娣峰悎绮惧害**锛氭帉鎻′簡bf16璁粌鍜孎lash Attention
11. **鍒嗗竷寮忚缁?*锛氱悊瑙ｄ簡DDP鍜孌eepSpeed
12. **鎺ㄧ悊浼樺寲**锛氬浼氫簡KV缂撳瓨鍜岄噰鏍风瓥鐣?
MiniMind鏄竴涓粷浣崇殑瀛︿範椤圭洰锛屽畠鐢ㄦ渶绠€娲佺殑浠ｇ爜灞曠ず浜嗙幇浠ｅぇ璇█妯″瀷鐨勬墍鏈夋牳蹇冩妧鏈€傚缓璁綘锛?
1. **鍔ㄦ墜瀹炶返**锛氶€愯闃呰浠ｇ爜锛岀悊瑙ｆ瘡涓嚱鏁扮殑浣滅敤
2. **淇敼瀹為獙**锛氳皟鏁村弬鏁帮紝瑙傚療妯″瀷琛屼负鐨勫彉鍖?3. **鎵╁睍寮€鍙?*锛氬皾璇曞湪鑷繁鐨勬暟鎹笂璁粌锛屾墿灞曟ā鍨嬪姛鑳?4. **娣卞叆鐮旂┒**锛氶槄璇荤浉鍏宠鏂囷紝鐞嗚В璁捐鑳屽悗鐨勭悊璁?
## 杩涗竴姝ュ涔犺祫婧?
1. **Transformer璁烘枃**锛?Attention Is All You Need"
2. **LLaMA璁烘枃**锛?LLaMA: Open and Efficient Foundation Language Models"
3. **RoPE璁烘枃**锛?RoFormer: Enhanced Transformer with Rotary Position Embedding"
4. **Flash Attention璁烘枃**锛?FlashAttention: Fast and Memory-Efficient Exact Attention with IO-Awareness"
5. **LoRA璁烘枃**锛?LoRA: Low-Rank Adaptation of Large Language Models"
6. **DPO璁烘枃**锛?Direct Preference Optimization: Your Language Model is Secretly a Reward Model"

绁濅綘瀛︿範鎰夊揩锛屽湪AI鐨勯亾璺笂涓嶆柇鎺㈢储锛?
